Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The markov_chains variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 585 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 145 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_585(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 585. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^145(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the markov_chains object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 640. The moduli space has dimension at most 650 with 5 connected components.

By induction on n, assuming the bound 640, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 650. QED.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the monotonicity property condition on markov_chains structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 69. The convergence follows from the monotonicity property estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 772. QED.

Theorem 214.997 (Recursion Lemma)

Statement: The markov_chains variety V defined by recursion lemma constraints admits a stratification into 361 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 490 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the recursion lemma decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_361(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 361. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^490(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Let the markov_chains structure S satisfy the conditions of conservation law framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 565. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 393 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the conservation law operator L on the markov_chains space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 565 for all k. By the conservation law theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 393. QED.

By the entropy bound framework applied to markov_chains structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 40/218 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The markov_chains operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 786. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 755 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_786(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 786. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^755(S, F) is trivial. QED.
